# Swarm audit of the research materials, 2026-10-29

Read-only audit of the reports, findings, pre-registration, methodology docs, legal memo, README and handoff at master `ff7c1179`, run 2026-10-29 16:30–18:00 MDT. Six checkers (numbers, stale claims, quotes and names, legal citations, pre-registration labels, cross-document consistency) and one 1992-record search ran in parallel; two adversarial verifiers then re-checked every CONFIRMED finding.

**624 findings.** 469 confirmed by the verifiers, 22 demoted, 3 refuted, 92 likely and 38 questions not put to the verifiers. Of the confirmed: 133 high, 265 medium, 71 low. 236 confirmed mechanical fixes are applied in this PR; 31 more sit in pre-registration documents or the signed amendment log and are held for the author; 202 confirmed findings need a judgment call and are listed, not changed.

Statuses: CONFIRMED (checker reproduced it and a verifier re-checked it), DEMOTED (verifier could not reproduce it or the fix is not mechanical), REFUTED (the claim is right), LIKELY and QUESTION (not verified). Line numbers are at `ff7c1179`.

## Coverage

**numbers.** Nenshi quote (user's request): checked in the media and in Hansard. Searches (standard and extended) turned up the Lethbridge Herald story of April 1, 2026 (lethbridgeherald.com/news/lethbridge-news/2026/04/01/amery-nenshi-spoke-about-alberta-electoral-boundaries-report/), which I saw only as search snippets. There, 'This had never been done before in Alberta history' is about the minority report from the UCP-appointed commissioners, not the committee route. The snippet says 'Nenshi said Tuesday', which would be March 31. A search limited to assembly.ab.ca found no Hansard instance. The April 16, 2026 Hansard PDF (docs.assembly.ab.ca/.../20260416_1000_01_han.pdf) came up in search with Nenshi's 'one and only final report' line but not this quote. Southern Alberta Newspapers paraphrase him calling the move 'unprecedented and undemocratic', which is not a quote. Verdict: the quote is real but was used out of context, so the C17 withdrawal is correct. No in-scope file still uses it. The only mention is C17 (report_academic.md:2716), which is accurate. WebFetch failed for lethbridgeherald.com, albertandpcaucus.ca and en.wikipedia.org; I did not retry. Those hosts would have to be allowed in the session's network settings to read the pages in full. Read in full or near-full: proposals/site_rewrite_2026-10-29/legal_framework.md, findings/procedural_analysis.md, docs/FINDINGS_BRIEF.md, SESSION_HANDOFF.md, preregistration/lane3_process_scorecard_draft.md, preregistration/november_2026_scoring_spec.md, findings/hansard_april16_motion19.md, findings/chair_recommendation_5_analysis.md (lines 40-127), findings/ensemble_chain1_duplication_note.md, findings/regional_swing_canonical_robustness.md, reports/public/report_public.md (lines 1-622), README.md (lines 100-200), docs/act_amendment_proposal.md (lines 1-146), findings/lunty_91_seat_preliminary.md (lines 1-60 and 195-219). reports/academic/report_academic.md: grep across the whole file plus targeted reads of lines 50-60, 243-300, 316-324, 520-541, 842, 902, 972-974, 1086-1110, 1315-1320, 1441, 1504-1560, 1598-1660, 1990-2060, 2229-2245, 2440-2455, 2496-2600, 2630, 2695-2745, 3146. All other in-scope findings/*.md, preregistration/*.md, analysis/methodology/*.md, docs/REPRODUCING.md, docs/data_sources.md and docs/ADAPTING_TO_OTHER_JURISDICTIONS.md: grep only, for committee, precedent, Chief Justice, Cassista, unanimous, R5(d), Lalande, s.12 and every headline number. **quotes-names.** NENSHI QUOTE (the user's request: grade it, or drop it if it can't be found; check media and Hansard). Verdict: the line is real in secondary coverage but cannot be used as a no-precedent claim about the committee. It is already dropped, and nothing in scope still uses it. Media: web search returned the Lethbridge Herald piece of April 1, 2026 ("Amery, Nenshi spoke about Alberta electoral boundaries report", https://lethbridgeherald.com/news/lethbridge-news/2026/04/01/amery-nenshi-spoke-about-alberta-electoral-boundaries-report/). In the snippet, Nenshi says the UCP-appointed commissioners issued a minority report with maps drawn to support the UCP, then: "This had never been done before in Alberta history, and the commission chair says the minority recommendations are..." (the snippet is cut off there). Another result says the minority 'for the first time' released competing maps. So the remark is about the minority report and predates Government Motion 37 (introduced April 16). Hansard: searches found no Hansard instance, and assembly.ab.ca could not be fetched. The repo's April 16 Hansard extract (findings/hansard_april16_motion19.md) quotes different Nenshi lines. The only in-scope mention left is C17 at reports/academic/report_academic.md:2716, which describes the withdrawal accurately. Search also turned up other Nenshi remarks calling the government's move 'unprecedented' or 'one of the only times in Canadian history'. These were not verified, are not used in the repo, and Alberta 1992 would contradict them anyway. WebFetch failed on lethbridgeherald.com, bowislandcommentator.com and en.wikipedia.org (outside the session's allowed network domains), so after that I used WebSearch only.
